DATA SOURCE
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Origin          : github.com/microsoft/presidio-research (main branch, MIT)

                    their own recognizers

                    written by the Presidio team - NOT authored by this project

  Note            : newlines in source text are displayed as \n
